# W1 structural fidelity: Architect contract and rework plan (2026-10-17)

Decision: `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` in `.ai/DECISIONS.md`. The owner rejected the combined W1 walkthrough; the verbatim verdict is `.agent/owner-W1-walkthrough-verdict-2026-10-17.md`. This is a layout correction, not a data or capability promotion. Scope is the 26 Site screens in the W1 walkthrough and W1 registry list; the registry also labels `lab` W1, but it is not one of the 26 requested screens and needs its own Lab comparison before a later owner session. The registry and its checker stay unchanged.

## Binding layout contract for W1 rework and W2+ UI

For every named screen, render the v69 panel **set, order, titles, relative prominence and size, grid, and visual style** from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, `mockups.css`, `screen-manifest.json`, and `frontend/docs/architecture/v69-rendered-panels.json`. Use the mock's page heading, compact metric, panel, two-column, timeline, badge, table and gap patterns; port missing tokens/patterns into `frontend/src/ui/tokens.css` and `primitives.css` or shared UI components. Existing type/colour tokens that already match should be reused. Compare the rendered mock at both widths; the JSON titles alone do not define spatial structure. The mock's illustrative values never become product values.

The panel title map below is exhaustive. Default title is exactly `v69-rendered-panels.json`. Approved plain-language changes are: `foundation:2` **Identity and operating obligations** for `Identity & operating obligations`; `dispatch:2` **Selected block Â· {actual selected interval}** for the mock's illustrative `14:20â€“14:35`; `readiness:2` **Gateway status Â· {actual gateway name or â€œNo gateway selectedâ€}** for `GW-MG-01`; `finding-evidence:1` **Evidence bundle Â· {actual Finding name or â€œSelected Findingâ€}** for `F-104`; `sld:1` **{actual site name} Â· {configured topology type, if stated}** for `Kobo Mini-grid Â· AC-coupled mini-grid`. Dynamic titles must use the selected read, never mock fixtures. An unknown dynamic value is labelled unknown in the same panel. No other title substitutions are approved here.

Each mock panel retains its slot when a selected read cannot support its content. Put the precise honest gap **inside that panel** (unavailable, refused, pending, stale, withdrawn, not assessed, absent, or proved zero as appropriate); never synthesize a value, omit the panel, or fill it with a different period's value. A page-level refusal or no-version state may state the refusal before the panel grid, but still renders the panel skeletons with in-panel gaps where the route and screen are valid. Place current app-only content within its owning mock panel or the named contextual Analysis/Evidence/History destination below. Remove the W1-UX leading answer cards and duplicate old panels after merging their supported answer, visible state/scope/as-of, and next action into the matching mock panel. No new leading card. Keep material limits visible beside the claim.

`D-2026-10-16-w1-ux` and `-w1-ux-disclosure` still govern contextual Analysis tabs, narrow exact-reference disclosures, plain words and honest claims. At 390 px the same Analysis destination stays accessible. A closed disclosure cannot hold substantive method or hide a material limit. `D-2026-10-17-w1-rail` and `-w1-ux-06` continue to govern navigation and the configuration/contact/condition distinction.

## Enforcement and review gate

| Gate | Required proof |
| --- | --- |
| Panel structure | Add a route-render test for **each of the 26 screen IDs**, with stocked and meaningful empty/refused variants. Read expected indexed titles from `v69-rendered-panels.json` plus the five approved dynamic/plain-language rules above; query only main-content panel headings, assert exact count and DOM order, and reject any extra leading card/panel before panel 1. Include the selected tab's panels, not hidden tabs/drawers or shell headings. Assert in-panel gap text for unsupported reads. A route with a valid screen identity must not turn into a wholly different error-card layout. |
| Visual evidence | Extend the existing W1 evidence capture tools to save **app and mock side by side** at 1440 and 390 px for every screen, with the same route state and visible panel sequence. Capture one supported and one significant missing/held state per area; log app URL, mock hash, fixture/read selection, revision, viewport, and image paths. Compare top fold, panel widths/heights, grid, density, typography, color, spacing and mobile flow. Where mock sample content differs, judge structure and truthful gap treatment. |
| Independent review | Reviewer checks the structure test, every capture pair and mock CSS patterns. **Any panel set/order/title/grid/prominence mismatch is High**, even if copy tests pass; a leading answer card is High. Unsupported claims and hidden material limits retain their existing severity. No owner session until automated checks and side-by-side review pass for all 26 at both widths; W1 remains unaccepted until the owner accepts the reworked screens. |

The title test is an exact structure guard, not a pixel test. Reviewers must judge relative sizes and visual rhythm from captures. Do not weaken a test to preserve an extra app panel; move its content to the owning slot or contextual destination.

# W2 cold-chain technical design â€” 2026-10-17

## 1. W2-00: ordinary source-to-evidence format

The active authenticated path uses **SourceEnvelopeV3**, not the older staged v1/v2 envelope: v3 names record families but refuses them, while S2 also refuses non-measurements (`contracts/assetops_contracts/source_envelope_v3.py:1-43,77-78,240-246`; `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/ledger.py:268-280`). Extend that path as schema **4**, `assetops.source-envelope`; retain v3's allocated `(gateway,source,epoch,sequence)` message identity, canonical JSON, UTC instants and exact numeric text. `source_envelope.py` v1/v2 and existing Stage artifacts remain byte-for-byte readable. The v4 parser accepts v4 only; a version dispatcher accepts v3 and v4. v3 receipts, dedup keys and accepted rows are untouched. W2-02 pins v4 in a new cold publication profile; never switch an existing profile in place (`contracts/assetops_contracts/source_envelope_v3.py:1-43,70-78`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:87-107`).

| v4 family / kind | Exact payload keys in addition to common v3 top-level fields | Rule |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `MEASUREMENT` | Exact v4 keys `family,measurement,value,unit,quality,unavailable_reason,session_id,connector_id`; the last two are explicit null for cold and non-session EV channels. v4 alone permits `STATE_AT_INSTANT`,`INTERVAL_MEAN`,`INTERVAL_DELTA`; delta is nonnegative `kWh` over a positive `[interval_start,interval_end)` and requires both EV IDs. Cold point Â°C uses `degC`; states use `state`; mean power uses `kW`. | Define a separate `V4_MEASUREMENTS` allowlist in `source_envelope_v4.py`; leave shared `ENVELOPE_MEASUREMENTS` and v3 parsing unchanged. | `EVENT` / `DOOR_OPEN`, `DOOR_CLOSE`, `START_REQUEST`, `START_RESULT`, `TRANSFER_RESULT`, `POWER_LOSS`, `POWER_RESTORED`, `MANUAL_LOG` | `family,record_kind,event_id,correlation_id,component_id,actor,outcome,unit,value,quality,session_id,connector_id`; the last two are explicit null. `event_id` and `correlation_id` required identifiers; `component_id` required except Site power events; `actor` = `CONTROLLER`,`SENSOR`,`OPERATOR`; `outcome` = `REQUESTED`,`SUCCEEDED`,`FAILED`,`OPEN`,`CLOSED`,`LOST`,`RESTORED`,`OBSERVED`,`UNKNOWN`, constrained per kind; `unit/value` either both null or a permitted quantitative pair (manual log `degC` only); `quality` = `GOOD` or `SUSPECT`. | An event is a witnessed public observation, not private disturbance truth. `MANUAL_LOG` needs registered operator source and stays contextual unless calibrated to the probe role. |

Kind-to-outcome allowlist is exact: `DOOR_OPENâ†’OPEN`, `DOOR_CLOSEâ†’CLOSED`, `START_REQUESTâ†’REQUESTED`, `START_RESULTâ†’SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, `TRANSFER_RESULTâ†’SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, `POWER_LOSSâ†’LOST`, `POWER_RESTOREDâ†’RESTORED`, `MANUAL_LOGâ†’OBSERVED`, `EV_SESSION_STARTâ†’STARTED`, `EV_SESSION_ENDâ†’COMPLETED`, `EV_SESSION_ABORTâ†’ABORTED`, `EV_CONNECTOR_FAULTâ†’FAULTED`, `EV_CONNECTOR_CLEARâ†’CLEARED`. All other pairs refuse `INVALID_PAYLOAD`. `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/`degC`; every other event requires both null. A record kind with no `session_id` still carries the key as null; `connector_id` is likewise explicit null for cold events.

Every v4 top-level object keeps the v3 exact keys; `message` keeps `message_id,sequence_epoch,sequence`, `source` and `mapping` keep v3 keys, `timing` keeps `source_time,published_at,interval_start,interval_end`. Events require null interval bounds; their source time is the observed transition, not a duration. For cold event `correlation_id` is the controller outage/attempt ID; a door pair shares its door-cycle ID. For EV, `correlation_id=session_id` on session events and a required fault episode ID on fault/clear. `event_id` is stable under retransmission and unique within `(site,source,record_kind)`; message ID remains transport identity. Canonical event payload contains explicit nulls for inapplicable fields. No arbitrary JSON detail, free text, Lab run ID, oracle, private cause, or stock identity. Event IDs generated from frozen scenario + public device channel + transition ordinal in Lab; physical adapters preserve vendor stable event ID or derive a documented hash from raw immutable event key. Cross-source event correlation is a claim input, never an acceptance dedup key.

### W2-00 implementation rulings (`D-2026-10-17-w2-00-answers`)

1. **Event equality.** In `source_envelope_v4.py`, define `event_identity_sha256` as SHA-256 of UTF-8 canonical JSON with exactly three keys: `source` (the complete v4 source object), `timing` (`source_time,interval_start,interval_end` only), and `payload` (the complete v4 payload). Compute it from the stored canonical `envelope_text` on collision reread; add no column. Exclude `schema,message,mapping,timing.published_at`, batch and publication metadata. The existing `envelope_sha256` remains the full canonical text digest for same-message comparison. For an existing event key, equal event identity is `DUPLICATE` with its first accepted ID even when message, mapping or publication time differs; unequal identity is `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT`/`CONFLICT`. `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` records `field='payload.event_id'`; `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT` records `field='payload.session_id'`. Message collision always wins. A distinct START at the same scoped connector/session is `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT` after the event-key check. `postgres_evidence_ledger.py` uses a per-ordinal savepoint for event/START unique violations, then rereads message, event, START in that order; unrelated integrity errors propagate. Tests change only transport fields versus source time, outcome and payload, and cover both double collisions and a race loser.
2. **Delta storage and refusals.** In migration `0020`, the CHECK predicate is `measurement='INTERVAL_DELTA'`, never `conversion_kind='INTERVAL_DELTA'`: conversion stays the binding's `IDENTITY` (or another explicitly supported conversion), and `INTERVAL_DELTA` is the v4 measurement kind. Use the exact delta and ID-pair CHECKs below (names `accepted_evidence_ev_delta_shape`, `accepted_evidence_measurement_ids`). For every non-delta MEASUREMENT, both IDs are null in `0020`; W2-05 may extend that rule only with a reviewed migration. In `source_envelope_v4.py`, wrong delta unit yields `EV_DELTA_UNIT_INVALID` at `payload.unit`, absent/nonpositive/misaligned bounds yields `EV_DELTA_INTERVAL_INVALID` at `timing.interval_start` or `timing.interval_end`, and negative or unavailable delta yields `EV_DELTA_VALUE_INVALID` at `payload.value`. Missing/null/invalid session or connector ID yields `CORRELATION_INVALID` at its payload field, including an absent key as a specific exception to generic `MISSING_FIELD`. A present but wrong bound connector yields `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` at `payload.connector_id` in `ledger.py`. All five are ordinal `REJECTED`, null conflict reference. Existing v3 parser and shared measurement allowlist stay unchanged.

   `CHECK (measurement IS DISTINCT FROM 'INTERVAL_DELTA' OR (family = 'MEASUREMENT' AND original_unit = 'kWh' AND normalized_unit = 'kWh' AND original_value_text IS NOT NULL AND normalized_value_text IS NOT NULL AND original_value_text ~ '^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)(\.[0-9]*[1-9])?$' AND normalized_value_text ~ '^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)(\.[0-9]*[1-9])?$' AND interval_start IS NOT NULL AND interval_end IS NOT NULL AND interval_start < interval_end AND interval_end = source_time AND session_id IS NOT NULL AND connector_id IS NOT NULL))`

   `CHECK (family <> 'MEASUREMENT' OR ((measurement = 'INTERVAL_DELTA' AND session_id IS NOT NULL AND connector_id IS NOT NULL) OR (measurement IS DISTINCT FROM 'INTERVAL_DELTA' AND session_id IS NULL AND connector_id IS NULL)))`
3. **Batch admission.** `ingestion_batch_v2.py` retains strict v3 parsing for ordinary schema-3 texts. For a text declaring schema 4, or schema 3 with `payload.family='EVENT'` or `payload.measurement='INTERVAL_DELTA'`, its header-only reader validates the JSON object and the exact `message` identity/order and `source`/`mapping` fields needed for Batch V2 homogeneity and batch ID; it does not validate payload, timing, schema version or canonical inner-envelope bytes. `ledger.py` dispatches each stored text by stated schema and records parser refusals per ordinal; schema-3 v4-only kinds yield `SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED` at `payload.family` or `payload.measurement`. Bad/missing header fields that prevent batch identity or scope checks remain whole-batch `INVALID_ENVELOPE` before a ledger ordinal exists. Once a valid header exists, malformed payload, timing and noncanonical text are ordinal rejections with `evidence_rejection` and `ingestion_disposition`; preserve the valid header's message ID on `Rejected`. Tests prove a mixed valid/invalid v4 batch completes with one disposition per ordinal and that v3 golden batch/receipt bytes remain fixed.
4. **Binding and profile home.** Extend only the immutable `assetops.acquisition-snapshot/1` `bindings` rows: optional `allowed_record_kinds: string[]`, `allowed_outcomes: {record_kind:string[]}`, `requires_correlation: boolean`, `requires_session: boolean`, `allowed_units: string[]`. `binding_alignment.py` serializes these when supplied; `gateway/snapshot.py` admits exactly these optional keys; `ledger.py` `BindingBasis` reads absent keys as `None` for v3. A v4 binding requires all five explicitly (measurement rows use `[]`, `{}`, `false`, `false`, and their one allowed unit; event rows use their closed kinds/outcomes, `true`, session requirement, and `[]` except manual-log `['degC']`). For EV session rows `requires_session=true`; connector fault/clear rows permit null session with `requires_session=false`. In `source_envelope_v4.py`, `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS` is the closed `(device_id,signal_id)` table in Â§4 plus the two reserved EV tuples, versioned as `simulator-lab-gateway-cold/1` and schema 4. S2 checks exact tuple, family, kind, outcome, role, unit and required correlation/session against both the closed table and pinned row; missing or divergent v4 mapping metadata yields ordinal `MAPPING_INCOMPATIBLE` at `mapping`, and a unit mismatch yields `UNIT_INCOMPATIBLE` at `payload.unit`. W2-00 owns the parser, table and snapshot compatibility reader. W2-01 publishes cold binding rows; W2-02 registers and emits the new cold runtime publication profile. Those runtime producer/profile steps are an explicit W2-00 deviation deferred to W2-02. W2-05b registers/emits its separately pinned EV profile; existing profiles never switch in place.
5. **Tenant and recompute boundaries.** The authenticated principal's tenant/Site selects the binding set before any ordinal decision; a binding registered only in another tenant yields whole-batch `CONFIGURATION_UNREGISTERED` at `mapping` with no accepted evidence or ordinal disposition. Once scope and header pass, per-ordinal parser/binding refusals are persisted. Keep the existing `dispatch` recompute job only for accepted v3 measurements. W2-00 enqueues no job for v4 records: no current worker understands cold or EV inputs. W2-03 adds `cold_chain` enqueue/worker for accepted cold roles/events; W2-05 adds the EV domain under its own reviewed contract. Test that v4 acceptance creates no dispatch job and v3 still does.
6. **Read ownership and proof.** Extend the Postgres route only: `evidence_api.py` validates `family=MEASUREMENT|EVENT` and `kind` from the v4 event allowlist (bad values 422), then passes both through `ledger_reads.py` to `postgres_evidence_ledger.py` `records_page`; `kind` filters `record_kind`, including an empty result when paired with `family=MEASUREMENT`. Include the seven named fields in `record_payload`, null on old v3 rows, and in Postgres list/detail queries. The legacy file-backed read route retains its current contract and rejects either new filter with 422 `FILTER_UNSUPPORTED` rather than silently ignoring it. Update both receipt conflict-count SQL locations and the Postgres rejection-list conflict split to include `MESSAGE_CONFLICT,EVENT_ID_CONFLICT,SESSION_ID_CONFLICT`; freeze a `MESSAGE_CONFLICT` Receipt V2 byte fixture before the change. W2-00 tests use real private Postgres `*_test` databases and may patch `basis_from_snapshot` with v4 `BindingBasis` entries reusing registered binding/asset IDs until W2-01/02 can publish them; this is test-only, never runtime admission evidence. Test snapshot optional-key parsing, all ordinal codes, old-null/read filters, tenant refusal, receipt bytes/counts and rule-8 probes on the final revision.

`0022` adds nullable `finding.subject_asset_id uuid` with scoped asset FK; relaxes `finding.generator_asset_id` to nullable; adds CHECK `(domain='mini_grid' AND generator_asset_id IS NOT NULL) OR (domain='cold_chain' AND subject_asset_id IS NOT NULL AND generator_asset_id IS NULL)`. Thus revision CHECKs can use their own stored domain without trusting a caller. `0025` widens both domain CHECKs with `'ev_charging'`. Existing `mini_grid` rows stay untouched apart from the additive revision domain backfill. `recompute_job.domain` has no SQL CHECK. `0022` adds the `(tenant_id,site_id,domain,subject_asset_id,anchor_start)` index and updates Finding store/read selection by domain. The shared fuel consequence CHECK remains intact. The `0020` accepted-event unique index is enforced in the ledger transaction; race losers get the conflict disposition above.

For EV session events, `accepted_evidence.asset_id` is the effective bound EV_CONNECTOR UUID and `connector_id` is its canonical public identifier; S2 resolves the equality before INSERT. Every accepted-evidence INSERT, including EVENT and EV START, retains `ON CONFLICT ON CONSTRAINT accepted_evidence_message_unique DO NOTHING`. For each ordinal, the ledger opens a savepoint around that INSERT. On a message-target `DO NOTHING` or a unique violation of `accepted_event_identity_unique` or `accepted_ev_start_unique`, it rolls back to the savepoint where needed and rereads under the same batch transaction in fixed order: message key, then event identity, then EV START identity. A matching message SHA returns `DUPLICATE`; a changed message SHA returns `MESSAGE_CONFLICT` even when the event or START key also collides. If the message key is free, a matching event SHA returns `DUPLICATE` and a changed event SHA returns `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT`; only if neither key matches does a distinct START collision return `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT`. Thus message wins a double collision, then event wins over session. Each conflict references the first accepted row for its winning key. A non-unique or unlisted unique violation propagates; never convert it to a typed conflict. Unique indexes arbitrate races; the reread is not an application precheck.

## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract

The existing `0009` Finding transition CHECK already admits the three cold transitions and stays intact. These corrections supersede the shorter migration rows above.

### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)

The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.

**A â€” topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.

**B â€” event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.

**C â€” thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.

If a diagnostic exists but its freshness is UNKNOWN, report `STALE` with limitation `Contact time cannot be verified`, never claim recent contact. This is contact, not equipment health. W2-01 publishes `bindings[].cadence_seconds=300` as the fixed `cold-roles-1` channel contract, not from stored binding rows or readings; W2-02's frozen `cold-publication/1` profile must declare the same 300-second sampling cadence and refuse a mismatch. ## 4. W2-02 causal producer and publication

Add `cold-thermal/1` `ModelSpec` and `cold-publication/1`; resolve frozen profile identity in `host/execution_adapter.py:113-140`, `host/lab_execution.py:1978-2015` and `simulator/assetops_simulator/kernel/execute.py:90-105` through a closed registry, with fuel/mini-grid identities preserved. Reject unknown pack/profile/parameter version before Draft. The kernel receives typed model laws and power ports, not a cold-specific branch. Lab freezes Foundation/binding IDs, scenario SHA, model/profile/observation versions and seed in run identity; Stage seals public v4 envelopes, registered Commit links publication, SQLite outbox sends authenticated Batch V2, and S2 alone creates accepted evidence (`host/lab_execution.py:2154-2164,2428-2501`; `.agent/architect-W2-design.md:55-66`).

**Time and state.** Fixed 1-minute UTC step; all updates use exact rationals/decimal text and a defined end-of-step publication order. State: chamber air temperature `T` degC, controller demand, compressor `OFF|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, grid/backup bus energized flags, generator `STOPPED|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, transfer position `GRID|BACKUP|OPEN`, door `OPEN|CLOSED`, probe `OK|FAILED`. Private forcing: ambient `Ta`, door schedule, grid loss, generator failure, probe failure; private truth never serializes into a product envelope. Initial `T=3Â°C`, `Ta=20Â°C`, door closed, compressor off, grid and bus on, generator stopped, transfer grid, probe OK. Per minute, `heat_kWh = (heat_leak_kW_per_C*(Ta-T) + door_open*door_heat_kWh*60)*1/60`; `cool_kWh = compressor_running_and_bus_powered*cooling_kW*1/60`; `T_next=T+(heat_kWh-cool_kWh)/thermal_capacity_kWh_per_C`. Defaults: thermal capacity `1.2 kWh/degC`, leak `0.08 kW/degC`, door heat `0.02 kWh/min` (stored as `1.2 kW` equivalent while open), cooling `3 kW`, compressor start delay 1 minute, generator start delay 2 minutes, transfer delay 1 minute, start timeout 5 minutes, control on `T>=4Â°C`, off `T<=2Â°C`, policy `[2,4]Â°C`, hysteresis 0.2Â°C, probe bound Â±0.1Â°C. Clamp no state: fail run if `T` outside `[-40,60]Â°C`, energy residual nonzero, negative capacity/power, or impossible transition. The standalone cold pack owns its generator and bus power port; W2-02 does not compose with mini-grid.

Grid loss opens transfer and deenergizes compressor bus; controller emits `START_REQUEST` only if backup enabled, generator reaches RUNNING after delay unless failed, transfer closes to BACKUP after delay if generator power exists, and compressor restarts after its own delay if controller still demands cooling. Failed start emits `START_RESULT/FAILED` at timeout; successful start emits `START_RESULT/SUCCEEDED` when running. Transfer result is emitted only if a position sensor is configured; absence stays absence. Door events are sensor transitions; door opening adds heat only while open. Probe failure emits one `UNAVAILABLE` reading with reason `SENSOR_FAILURE` if the profile publishes it, then no numeric sample; reporting-path loss emits no envelope. Device measurement is `T + fixed observation bias` (default 0), rounded to 0.1Â°C by half-even rule; no private cause field. Controller and power events use observed public transitions and stable correlation ID.

All six `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` use `cold-scenario/1`, UTC run 2026-10-06 19:00â€“20:00 with a closing-boundary sample at 20:00, seed 20417, one standalone `cold-thermal/1` pack, and no mini-grid composition. The selected evidence/assessment window is half-open `[19:00,20:05)`, containing exactly 13 scheduled five-minute points from 19:00 through 20:00; no 20:05 point is included. Assets are `room-1`, `probe-1`, `door-1`, `generator-1`, `grid-1`, `bus-1`, `compressor-1`; generator backup enabled. Base initial state is T=3.00Â°C, ambient=20Â°C, door CLOSED, compressor OFF, grid/bus POWERED, generator STOPPED, transfer GRID, probe OK; excursion-derived cases override initial T to 3.80Â°C. Each row lists the entire non-default forcing, ordered by `(time,kind,asset)`; observation faults occur after a valid world transition and cannot bypass `PhysicallyUnacceptable` guards.

| Pack | UTC forcing `(time,kind,asset/connector,value)`; door schedule | Public assertion in `[19:00,20:05)` |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `normal` | `(19:05,GRID_LOSS,grid-1,LOST)`, `(19:35,GRID_RESTORE,grid-1,AVAILABLE)`; ambient override 10Â°C, door CLOSED throughout. | Request 19:05, success 19:07, transfer 19:08. `cold-publication/1` emits chamber `STATE_AT_INSTANT degC` every 5 minutes including end; grid/generator/compressor/bus states on transitions and 5-minute heartbeat; compressor `INTERVAL_MEAN kW` per aligned 5-minute slot; door/start/transfer/power events on transition. `simulator/assetops_simulator/observation/transform.py` owns `cold-observation/1` hold/drop/fault transform variants, zero bias and default 3-second latency before Draft. The model retains one calibrated primary chamber probe per room; delete the two-primary-probe disagreement rule. Contradictory distinct GOOD controller outcomes for one attempt cause `CONTRADICTORY`. `cold-door-heat` property unit is `kWh/min`, with 0.02 per open minute; the equation's `*60` converts it to kW before its one-minute integration. The Lab persisted-state validator uses the `cold-thermal/1` finite vocabularies above, not the mini-grid generator lifecycle vocabulary; extend the closed identity/profile registries. `Role.value_kind` adds `EVENT`, and EVENT bindings use `conversion='IDENTITY'`, with typed outcome allowlist and no numeric conversion. Foundation controls stay NUMBER, not a new `Role.value_kind`.

The closed cold mapping uses `(device_id,signal_id)`: `(chamber-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(ambient-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(grid-meter,state)` state point, `(generator-controller,state)` state point, `(compressor-controller,state)` state point, `(backup-bus-meter,state)` state point, `(compressor-meter,power)` kW interval mean, `(door-sensor,event)` door events, `(generator-controller,start-event)` request/result events, `(transfer-controller,event)` transfer result, `(grid-meter,event)` loss/restored events, `(operator-log,temperature-event)` manual log. Each tuple is a separate versioned mapping entry with only its listed kinds, values and cadence. W2-00 also reserves EV entries `(charger-controller,session-event)` for session start/end/abort and `(connector-controller,fault-event)` for fault/clear, with connector/session identity rules above; W2-05b supplies the EV pack and its own frozen mapping version. The same signal ID on a different device is not an implicit alias (`simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:22-46,87-107`).

## 5. W2-03 assessment, episodes and Findings

Analytic IDs `cold-excursion/1`, `cold-start-response/1`. Input selection is accepted evidence only, fixed half-open `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at` cutoff on server `received_at`/decision time, effective Foundation/binding versions, and source gate. Include exact accepted IDs and SHA, configuration IDs, model version, window/cutoff in sorted basis SHA. A reading is usable only if GOOD, calibrated, mapped to the room, and clock-attested; SUSPECT is context. Expected five-minute points: coverage = usable distinct scheduled timestamps / scheduled timestamps, capped at 1; `>=0.8` needed for an interval claim, but no gap >10 minutes may be bridged. Latest point must be <=10 minutes old at read time for a **current** state; historical window conclusion can remain bounded with explicit as-of. A failed worker or newer accepted relevant evidence marks prior projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new immutable revision is written.

Excursion begins after two consecutive GOOD observations above upper band (or below lower band) at least 5 minutes apart; return requires two consecutive observations inside band by at least 0.2Â°C. The start is bounded by `[last in-band, first out-of-band]`; return by `[last out-of-band, first stable in-band]`; duration low = max(0, earliest return - latest start), high = latest return - earliest start, computed only across continuously covered spans. Initial out-of-band sample with no prior in-band gives unknown start; window end without return gives open episode and no finite high. `degree_minutes` bounds use observed rectangles only between adjacent usable samples <=10 minutes apart: low uses min adjacent exceedance, high max adjacent exceedance times interval minutes; add no unobserved gap area. Peak is the maximum **observed** value. An exposure Finding requires 15-minute lower duration and coverage >=0.8; otherwise `POSSIBLE` or `UNKNOWN`, never â€œno excursionâ€ from missing samples. In-band fully covered is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` scoped to window, not equipment health.

Start chain groups by accepted `correlation_id`, generator asset and outage window. Require ordered grid LOST â†’ START_REQUEST â†’ START_RESULT â†’ generator RUNNING/FAILED â†’ optional TRANSFER_RESULT â†’ bus POWERED/UNPOWERED â†’ compressor state â†’ probe response; tolerance is source-clock bound <=30 seconds for event ordering, with timeout from effective Foundation (default 5 minutes). A failed automatic start is `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with request, explicit failed result or timeout plus complete generator state coverage through timeout, and no contradictory success; missing request/result/state is `UNKNOWN_STEP`. A transfer-position claim requires accepted transfer event; bus energization alone proves only bus state. Cooling failure cannot be assigned from temperature alone. Emit claim levels `OBSERVED`, `BOUNDED`, `POSSIBLE`, `UNKNOWN`, `CONTRADICTORY`; explanations list exact missing/excluded records and claim ceiling.

Episode key = SHA-256 of `(tenant,site,kind,subject room/generator asset,first accepted qualifying crossing/request event ID)`; retain anchor when late evidence adjusts boundaries, append `cold_episode_revision` and corresponding FindingRevision with transition `SUPPORT_CHANGED` or `BASIS_REFRESHED`. Two episodes split only after two stable in-band points (excursion) or a new start request (backup). If late evidence invalidates the anchor, append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` and create a new episode with `split_from` relation; never rewrite old. Separate excursion and start-response Findings can cite the same event but share one `overlap_group` for money. Use existing S4-05 exact accepted-ID provenance and source classification (`backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:33-87`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

## 6. W2-04 consequence, reads and UI

`PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is a bounded thermal exposure indicator in `degC_min`, **not product quantity, spoilage or loss**. `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value` (currency/unit) and a product-specific reviewed `cold_exposure_rule` would be needed for a future valued risk scenario; W2 creates no default and still forbids an amount without an approved rule. `RECORDED_REPAIR` amount requires exact accepted work/cost record, currency, time and non-overlap; no such source exists in W2, so it is `UNPRICED`. All consequences pin FindingRevision, episode revision, accepted IDs, assumption revision IDs, model/formula version, source gate and overlap group. Missing/contradictory probe or policy, stale projection, mixed/unknown provenance, undeclared stock or cost â†’ `UNPRICED`/`WITHHELD` with null amount. Never sum backup and excursion exposure; no zero amount as stand-in for unknown. The existing LR current-money and S4-05 source gates still apply (`backend/migrations/versions/0010_financial_assumptions.py:1-30`; `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-69`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

All routes below use Site code, authenticated tenant scope, UTC `from,to` (end exclusive), optional UTC `known_at` (default current committed watermark), max 31-day window, and return `{site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance,limitations,...}`. `watermark` is latest included ingestion disposition `decided_at` plus assessment revision ID; a selected historical cutoff cannot claim today's currentness. Lists use opaque `(time,id)` cursor, `limit` default 50 max 200 and `{items,next_cursor}`. Shared refusal envelope `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 unauthenticated, 403 wrong tenant/source-gated disclosure, 404 Site/record not found or wrong type, 409 unresolved selected revision or recompute conflict, 422 malformed/window/range/unknown parameter, 503 ledger/projection unavailable; a valid missing claim returns 200 with `UNKNOWN`/null, never 404 or a fabricated zero. Existing evidence/Finding paths continue to serve exact links (`backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:157-161`; `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:102-189`).

## 7. Slice proof fixtures and acceptance

### Writer guard and money trigger contract

Rule 8 in `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1`: add `cold_episode,cold_episode_revision,cold_episode_evidence` to `$assessTables` before `$assessInserts` is derived; add `cold_consequence_revision,cold_consequence_evidence,cold_consequence_work,cold_consequence_work_attempt` to `$consequenceTables`. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone inserts episode and analytic/assessment rows; `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts Findings. `assessment_worker.py` owns one caller-supplied transaction spanning both adapters and job completion. `postgres_consequence_store.py` alone inserts consequence rows and append-only work attempts; migration triggers alone insert work. The consequence writer leases with an advisory transaction lock per work ID, appends one `SUCCEEDED` attempt atomically with consequence/links or a `FAILED` attempt with error code, and never updates/deletes work or attempts. Cross-writer checks protect all four tables. The ledger alone inserts event evidence, rejection and disposition in one transaction. Every accepted-evidence INSERT uses only the message constraint conflict target; event and START unique violations are handled by per-ordinal savepoint rollback and ordered reread (message, event, session) as in Â§2. No `DO UPDATE` permission is added.

Set `$ledgerConflictAllowed` to the double-quoted PowerShell regex `"(?i)\bON\s+CONFLICT\s+ON\s+CONSTRAINT\s+(?:batch_refusal_identity|ingestion_batch_external_unique|accepted_evidence_message_unique)\s+DO\s+NOTHING\b"`; retain its "three" target error text. Do not allow an event partial-index conflict target. `ledger_reads.py` uses `kind IN ('MESSAGE_CONFLICT','EVENT_ID_CONFLICT','SESSION_ID_CONFLICT')` in both receipt count locations (lines 232 and 376), retaining existing `MESSAGE_CONFLICT` objects and bytes. No migration touches old Receipt V2 rows.

Cold money work is triggered by appended cold FindingRevision or episode revision, relevant assumption publication or source-gate change; a migration trigger inserts append-only dirty `(site,window,domain='cold_chain')` work with unique dirty key and `DO NOTHING` duplicate suppression. `financials/recompute.py` runs the consequence worker; it selects unfinished work lacking a successful attempt, locks per work ID, selects the exact settled assessment/Finding basis, writes immutable consequence/evidence rows through `postgres_consequence_store.py`, and appends a `SUCCEEDED` attempt in the same transaction. Failure appends a `FAILED` attempt with an error code, leaving work retryable; no work status UPDATE exists. `tools/writer_guard_probes.py` gains pass probes for each owner/table, message-target conflict and attempt append, and fail probes for runtime work INSERT, foreign writer, immutable UPDATE/DELETE, `DO UPDATE`, and event partial-index conflict target. Include double-collision probes: message+event must choose message; event+session with a free message must choose event. `configuration-persistence.ps1` counts probes and fails closed when an owner is absent. `overlap_group` is SHA-256 of canonical `(tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id)` prefixed `cold:`; excursion and backup consequences for that room/window share it.

### Response vocabulary

Every object includes `as_of: UTC timestamp|null` and `limitation: string|null`. Common `currentness.state` is exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`; `source_provenance` is `{classification: ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|AUTHORED|MIXED|UNKNOWN, source_ids: string[], evidence_ids: UUID[], basis_sha256: string|null}`; root `limitations` is `string[]`. `start_bounds,end_bounds,episode_bounds` are `{earliest: UTC timestamp|null,latest: UTC timestamp|null}`; `duration_bounds,degree_minute_bounds,quantity_bounds,amount_bounds` are `{low: decimal|null,high: decimal|null}`. `gaps` is `[{start:UTC timestamp,end:UTC timestamp,reason:MISSING_PROBE|SUSPECT|CLOCK_UNATTESTED|CONTRADICTORY|UNBOUND,expected_points:int,received_points:int}]`, with half-open bounds. `coverage` is a decimal in `[0,1]` plus `{known_points:int,expected_points:int}` on temperature/evidence cards.

| Field | Exact shape |
| --- | --- |
| `status` | `{state: OBSERVED_EXCURSION|NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,claim_level:OBSERVED|BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,as_of,limitation}` |
| `objective` | `{text:string,policy_low_deg_c:decimal|null,policy_high_deg_c:decimal|null,room_asset_id:UUID|null,as_of,limitation}` |
| `temperature_summary` | `{latest_deg_c:decimal|null,latest_at:UTC timestamp|null,coverage:{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal},episode_count:int,claim_level:BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,as_of,limitation}` |
| `backup_summary` | `{attempts:int,observed_success:int,observed_failure:int,unknown:int,latest_chain_id:UUID|null,as_of,limitation}` |
| `causal_strip` | `[{step:GRID|START_REQUEST|START_RESULT|GENERATOR|TRANSFER|BUS|COMPRESSOR|PROBE,state:OBSERVED|MISSING|CONTRADICTORY,at:UTC timestamp|null,evidence_id:UUID|null,as_of,limitation}]` |
| `findings` | `{count:int,items:[{finding_id:UUID,revision_id:UUID,title:string,claim_level:string,link:string}],as_of,limitation}` |
| `needs_attention` | `[{code:PROBE_GAP|START_FAILURE|TRANSFER_UNKNOWN|RECOMPUTE_PENDING|POLICY_MISSING,text:string,link:string|null,as_of,limitation}]` |
| `consequence_summary` | `{state:UNPRICED|WITHHELD|RECORDED,quantity:{low:decimal|null,high:decimal|null,unit:'degC_min'},amount:{low:decimal|null,high:decimal|null,currency:string|null},overlap_group:string|null,as_of,limitation}` |
| `capabilities[].claim` | `TEMPERATURE_POINT|EXCURSION_BOUNDS|BACKUP_START_RESULT|TRANSFER_POSITION|COOLING_RESPONSE|PRODUCT_EXPOSURE`; `state` = `SUPPORTED|LIMITED|UNAVAILABLE|CONTRADICTORY`; `required_roles,missing_roles` = `string[]`; `coverage` = `{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal}`. |
| Foundation states | `readiness.state=READY_TO_REPORT|CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE`; `commissioning.state=CONFIRMED|UNCONFIRMED`; `contact.state=RECENT|STALE|NEVER`; `messages` are typed role-specific strings above. |

All manifests live under `contract-fixtures/w2/cold/` as canonical JSON with `schema=cold-replay-manifest/1`, scenario/profile/model/observation SHA, seed, Site/Foundation/binding IDs, UTC half-open selected window `[19:00,20:05)`, expected public envelope and batch SHA, receipt dispositions, accepted evidence IDs, assessment basis and bounded assertions. .ai/DECISIONS.md:3849:Resolve `A-edge-diagnostics` with a separate authenticated GatewayDiagnosticsV1 report and append-only central `gateway_diagnostic` table. The gateway sends local sequence, observed time, source-contact and queue/disk/clock diagnostics independently of telemetry; the server records receipt time, enforces gateway/site scope and idempotent identity, and derives freshness from its receive time and declared cadence. Diagnostics are operational state, never AcceptedEvidence or an asset-health claim. Sections 1, 3 and 4 of the technical design define the table, wire and API.
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3927:`GatewayDiagnosticsV1.observed_at`, local contact/pressure observation times and central `received_at` use real UTC clocks and remain separate from the virtual publication clock. For SIMULATED evidence, `published_at - source_time` is a simulated reporting delay; `received_at - published_at` is not a physical transfer-lag measure when the clocks use different timelines. S2-02 must label these domains, display source/published/received instants with provenance and derive central diagnostic freshness from server receive time, not virtual Lab time. A future LIVE gateway uses its own real clock and does not inherit this Lab control.
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3931:Persist at most one immutable diagnostic per declared real-UTC cadence slot; a repeat returns its exact stored identity and bytes, and a clock earlier than the latest slot refuses. State changes within a slot are visible in the local read immediately but reach the wire in the next slot; the server uses receive time and cadence to decide central freshness. `queue_count` is the number of `STAGED|READY|IN_FLIGHT` envelopes, sealed or unbatched, excluding holds and dead letters; queue age uses their real-UTC outbox creation time and must remain real UTC when Lab V3 `published_at` becomes virtual. The local read retains sealed/unbatched/held/refused/dead-letter breakdown. `mapping_error_count` is the number of distinct `mapping_hold` rows with kind in `{NO_ACTIVE_MAPPING, UNKNOWN_CHANNEL, SOURCE_NOT_PERMITTED, OUTSIDE_VALIDITY, UNIT_INCOMPATIBLE, INVALID_ENUM}`; it is not the total held-report count. Contact is gateway real-UTC observation of a public report that resolves to a permitted source, even on replay or later hold; never-heard remains null. For a SIMULATED gateway its provenance is `OBSERVED_SIMULATED_REPORT`, never proof of physical controller contact or source health. S2-02 carries these meanings into authenticated receipt and central presentation without adding cause or private Lab fields to V1.
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3951:The credential binds the gateway identity, not a frozen per-credential source list. On **every** upload request, check current gateway enablement and immutable Site affiliation. For Batch V2, also require every declared source to be currently enabled and affiliated with that gateway; a disabled gateway/source refuses even a byte-identical upload retry before idempotency lookup. For gateway diagnostics, verify every named source's registered gateway/Site affiliation, but permit a disabled source's historical contact to remain visible as disabled; disabling one source must not silence the gateway's operational report. Separately validate each evidence envelope against its immutable originally registered Foundation/binding/source-time basis; current source authorization neither rewrites nor substitutes that accepted-as basis. A replacement credential may rotate without changing sealed bytes, batch IDs or diagnostic identities; revoking the old verifier takes effect on the next request. Phase 1 may use an injected authenticated-principal port; network endpoints remain closed until this credential and TLS boundary exists. Mutual TLS client certificates and a separate token issuer remain deferred pilot alternatives, not implicit fallbacks.
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3957:Pilot bearer rotation revokes prior credential verifiers atomically when the new verifier is committed, with no overlap. The new secret must first be durably placed in a verified protected, versioned gateway file; a file/permission failure must leave the old credential active and the gateway/sources no more enabled than before. Do not overwrite the only usable old secret before successful issue; on Windows, verify an ACL restricting read to the operator and gateway process identity or refuse provisioning rather than claiming POSIX-only mode bits protected it. The central diagnostic is `CURRENT` through two declared-cadence periods from server receipt, then `STALE`, and `UNKNOWN` until a first receipt; this bounds slot-edge jitter without asserting device health. Pilot gateway upload uses direct TLS with the ASGI HTTPS scheme; forwarded scheme headers are not upload authority. A gateway diagnostic with malformed bytes or identity conflict receives an audited 422/409, while wrong path/claimed scope or foreign source receives 403 without a Site ledger row. The provisioning CLI remains development-tenant-only; its OS-user actor is labelled local operator context, not a verified tenant-user identity.
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3961:The gateway must refuse an upload credential file whose effective Windows DACL grants broader access than the operator/gateway process identity; provisioning-time verification alone is insufficient for an arbitrary configured path. Amend rotation so the verified unique versioned file is the final issued path, without a fallible post-commit rename: file/ACL failure before verifier commit leaves the old verifier and enablement intact, DB failure removes only the unissued new file, and successful commit immediately revokes the old verifier. Switch gateway configuration explicitly to the returned new path. Terminal publication uses `PUBLICATION_GAP` with schedule `TERMINAL_GAP` and refuses before either clock moves. A rejected/conflicting envelope is placed in a source-time window by its own parseable source time; unreadable-time rejections remain on the unwindowed route and are counted separately. Authenticated whole-batch refusals stay operational audit, not per-record Site Evidence. Until S3 computes dispatch, overlapping pending/leased/failed recompute jobs mean `STALE_PROJECTION`; no job means `NO_PENDING_DISPATCH`, never `CURRENT`. These decisions are Architect contracts, not owner Evidence acceptance.

.ai/DECISIONS.md:4048:**Projection status and cutover.** `NO_PENDING_DISPATCH` in the S2 Evidence read means only that no S2 job is outstanding; it is never a current assessment. Once S3 owns Performance, use `UNASSESSED` when no completed assessment exists, `PENDING` for a first run in progress, `CURRENT` only when a completed projection's input/source watermark matches accepted evidence and no overlapping dirty/leased/failed job remains, `STALE_PROJECTION` when a previous result exists but any such job or watermark mismatch remains, and `FAILED` when computation failed without a usable current result. Preserve prior revision links under stale/failed status and show job/error reason; no state silently means healthy, Necessary or zero. Reconcile the Evidence projection display to these truthful states after S3 activation. Before switching Performance reads to Postgres, compare representative file-backed dispatch claims against the same public evidence/configuration basis and the new model, recording matched outcomes, bounded intentional rule differences, lineage and exceptions; no private truth or stale JSON becomes product authority. If comparable baseline evidence is unavailable, record the exception and keep the affected read cutover closed rather than claim parity.

.ai/DECISIONS.md:4150:`SiteWindowSummary` is a tenant/Site-scoped, selected half-open UTC window read/projection over committed public records. It identifies the exact selected S3 assessment revision and its source/configuration/evidence basis, eligible S4 Finding revisions and any bounded financial consequence revisions, with accepted-source and analytic watermarks, model version, built time, coverage and a typed freshness reason. A user date change selects a new window; reload preserves the requested window and pinned detail IDs. Build atomically from a consistent committed basis or derive an equivalent scoped read; late accepted evidence, analytic/model/configuration changes and consequence work invalidate/rebuild affected windows. A projection is CURRENT only when its accepted-source basis matches the ledger and relevant analytic/consequence work is complete. Preserve old exact revision links when a new result is pending or stale. `UNASSESSED`/Unknown means no completed assessment or insufficient input, `PENDING` means first computation or queued work with no current result, `STALE_PROJECTION` means an earlier result exists but its watermark/basis lags or work is dirty, and `FAILED` names failed work without a usable current result. None implies Healthy, zero cost or no Findings.
.ai/DECISIONS.md:4187:Decision: `D-2026-10-15-s601-reopen`. For `reserve-trial-1`, a newer FindingRevision of the same canonical Finding must show earlier same-Site Actions with their pinned origin revisions, application/outcome states and exact links, even when live recomputation is `FOUNDATION_BASIS_STALE`. An earlier Action for the same battery asset and reserve field blocks a new acceptance while application is pending (including failed attempts awaiting retry) or applied with verification pending. # Architect ruling - W1-UX-07 full operator rail (2026-10-17)

The owner chose the full v69 menu with unbuilt entries greyed out and labelled "Not available yet". This ruling authorizes that narrow exception to the earlier live-items-only rail rule. It does not authorize placeholder pages, routes, inferred site selection, or new capability claims. The W1-UX-07 implementer changes code and tests; this document is the decision, not an implementation.

## Inventory and destinations

Render the following groups, root labels, and order exactly as `ROOTS` and `rail()` in `frontend/mockups/v69/screens.js`. Use `OPERATIONS`, not the current `OPERATE`. Group labels remain visible even when all their roots are unavailable.

| Group | Root | W1 treatment |
| --- | --- | --- |
| OPERATIONS | Portfolio | Unavailable |
| OPERATIONS | Sites | Live link to `/sites` |
| OPERATIONS | Assets | Unavailable |
| INTELLIGENCE | Findings | Live link to `/findings` |
| WORK | Actions | Unavailable |
| WORK | Incidents | Unavailable |
| WORK | Maintenance | Unavailable |
| WORK | Operational records | Unavailable |
| ASSURANCE | Evidence | Unavailable |
| BUSINESS | Financials | Unavailable |
| ADMIN | Settings | Unavailable |

Only Sites and Findings have cross-site index routes that can own these roots today. A site-scoped Asset, Action, Evidence, Financials, Work, or other screen does not make its root live: the rail cannot choose a site or object silently. Promote another root only in the slice that supplies and reviews a truthful cross-site destination, with the route and tests changed together. The inventory does not itself pass the grammar ownership test for a future live destination.

## Unavailable entry contract

Render each unavailable root as text in a list item, with the same icon slot and label as a live root, but visibly muted with sufficient readable contrast. Show the plain visible status `Not available yet` with each entry; do not rely on color, hover, a tooltip, or an icon to communicate it. The accessible name includes both the root label and that status. A noninteractive element with `aria-disabled="true"` is acceptable if it is announced consistently; do not use a disabled button. It has no `href`, `to`, click handler, route, pointer affordance, or tab stop. Tab and Shift+Tab move among live links and other real controls; Enter and Space on an unavailable row do nothing because the row is not focusable. Keep icons decorative to assistive technology.

No per-item reason is needed in W1. A future short reason may be shown only if it is an exact, evidenced product limitation, in plain words, and does not imply an available route or a temporary runtime outage. The standard status must remain visible. Never attach a `title`-only explanation. This rail treatment is an owner-directed inventory exception, not a general change to the rule that unsupported Site tabs and subtabs are absent.

## Workspace footer and Lab boundary

Use the v69 `WORKSPACE` footer placement for the `Simulator Lab` link only when `simulator_lab.enabled` is true and `/simulator-lab` is registered. It is a workspace utility link, not a seventh operator group or operator root; preserve the existing separation between operator and Lab shells. With the gate off, omit the Lab link and any Lab label or hint. Do not duplicate the current workspace utility link in the same rendered shell. Keep the route and link under the same gate and verify both gate states.

Omit `Demo workspace / 3 sites - illustrative records` for W1. The site count and illustrative status are mock data and cannot be asserted for a persisted tenant. A later footer may show a tenant/workspace label or count only from an authoritative current read, with loading, error, and empty states that do not invent a value.

## Guard, tests, and narrow screen

Amend `tools/checks/navigation-truthfulness.ps1` rather than bypassing it. Keep its parameterless Site route bans, identified Site route proof, legacy Foundation address rule, and Site tab/subtab checks. Those tab rules still reject disabled affordances and labelled-in-place absent capabilities. Add an operator-rail-specific invariant over the actual inventory/rendering contract: exactly the six groups and eleven labels above in order; exactly two live destinations (`Sites` -> `/sites`, `Findings` -> `/findings`); each live target registered in the operator route table and rendering a truthful frame; every other root has no destination or navigation handler. A typo, missing route, accidental `to`/`href`, or an unknown root must fail, not silently pass. Prefer a typed discriminated item model so unavailable entries cannot carry `to`; make the guard fail closed if it can no longer parse or locate the inventory. Do not weaken the old checks to accept disabled Site tabs.

Add component/route tests for order, all six headers, visible and accessible unavailable status, no links or keyboard activation for the nine unavailable roots, and successful navigation from both live roots. Check both Lab gate states, including no dead Lab link with the gate off. Include guard mutation fixtures or equivalent negative tests for an unavailable root accidentally gaining a destination and a live root pointing to a missing route. An unregistered route must never be "validated" by the catch-all `Page not available` frame.

At 390 px, the rail may move above the content as the existing narrow shell does, but all six groups, eleven roots, statuses, and the gated workspace footer remain readable in DOM order without horizontal page overflow. Use a single-column or bounded wrapping layout that keeps each label with its status; do not hide unavailable rows in an inaccessible overflow strip. The primary page content and live links must remain reachable by touch and keyboard. Verify at 390 px in the browser, including the longest label `Operational records`, status wrapping, focus order, and gate-on/off footer. The 1440 px rail preserves v69 grouping and order.

## Relationship to prior decisions

This is a scoped owner-directed amendment to the 2026-09-13 navigation rule and `D-2026-10-02-grammar-ownership-test`: the full root inventory is visible, while only truthful owned cross-site destinations are links. `D-2026-10-16-w1-ux` still gives truthful state and material limits priority over mockup fidelity. The G-01 missing-claim/orphan-scan boundary remains: `Not available yet` here describes a missing cross-site destination, not a claim that a site has no evidence or data. If the orphan scan sees these new literals, exempt only the identified rail status element with a reviewed reason or register its specific placement; do not globally suppress that phrase or weaken detection on product panels. This ruling does not promote W1 panels or record owner acceptance; the combined W1 walkthrough remains the visual checkpoint.

Every slice must land its affected route structure tests and 1440/390 comparison evidence before review. F5 confirms all 26 pass together, then the owner repeats the combined W1 walkthrough against the revised screens. The missing reads above are separate product/backend work; this rework must not fill them with simulated truth or mock values.

 succeeded in 3806ms:
Analytic IDs `cold-excursion/1`, `cold-start-response/1`. Input selection is accepted evidence only, fixed half-open `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at` cutoff on server `received_at`/decision time, effective Foundation/binding versions, and source gate. Include exact accepted IDs and SHA, configuration IDs, model version, window/cutoff in sorted basis SHA. A reading is usable only if GOOD, calibrated, mapped to the room, and clock-attested; SUSPECT is context. Expected five-minute points: coverage = usable distinct scheduled timestamps / scheduled timestamps, capped at 1; `>=0.8` needed for an interval claim, but no gap >10 minutes may be bridged. Latest point must be <=10 minutes old at read time for a **current** state; historical window conclusion can remain bounded with explicit as-of. A failed worker or newer accepted relevant evidence marks prior projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new immutable revision is written.

Excursion begins after two consecutive GOOD observations above upper band (or below lower band) at least 5 minutes apart; return requires two consecutive observations inside band by at least 0.2Â°C. The start is bounded by `[last in-band, first out-of-band]`; return by `[last out-of-band, first stable in-band]`; duration low = max(0, earliest return - latest start), high = latest return - earliest start, computed only across continuously covered spans. Initial out-of-band sample with no prior in-band gives unknown start; window end without return gives open episode and no finite high. `degree_minutes` bounds use observed rectangles only between adjacent usable samples <=10 minutes apart: low uses min adjacent exceedance, high max adjacent exceedance times interval minutes; add no unobserved gap area. Peak is the maximum **observed** value. An exposure Finding requires 15-minute lower duration and coverage >=0.8; otherwise `POSSIBLE` or `UNKNOWN`, never â€œno excursionâ€ from missing samples. In-band fully covered is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` scoped to window, not equipment health.

Start chain groups by accepted `correlation_id`, generator asset and outage window. Require ordered grid LOST â†’ START_REQUEST â†’ START_RESULT â†’ generator RUNNING/FAILED â†’ optional TRANSFER_RESULT â†’ bus POWERED/UNPOWERED â†’ compressor state â†’ probe response; tolerance is source-clock bound <=30 seconds for event ordering, with timeout from effective Foundation (default 5 minutes). A failed automatic start is `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with request, explicit failed result or timeout plus complete generator state coverage through timeout, and no contradictory success; missing request/result/state is `UNKNOWN_STEP`. A transfer-position claim requires accepted transfer event; bus energization alone proves only bus state. Cooling failure cannot be assigned from temperature alone. Emit claim levels `OBSERVED`, `BOUNDED`, `POSSIBLE`, `UNKNOWN`, `CONTRADICTORY`; explanations list exact missing/excluded records and claim ceiling.

Episode key = SHA-256 of `(tenant,site,kind,subject room/generator asset,first accepted qualifying crossing/request event ID)`; retain anchor when late evidence adjusts boundaries, append `cold_episode_revision` and corresponding FindingRevision with transition `SUPPORT_CHANGED` or `BASIS_REFRESHED`. Two episodes split only after two stable in-band points (excursion) or a new start request (backup). If late evidence invalidates the anchor, append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` and create a new episode with `split_from` relation; never rewrite old. Separate excursion and start-response Findings can cite the same event but share one `overlap_group` for money. Use existing S4-05 exact accepted-ID provenance and source classification (`backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:33-87`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

## 6. W2-04 consequence, reads and UI

`PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is a bounded thermal exposure indicator in `degC_min`, **not product quantity, spoilage or loss**. `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value` (currency/unit) and a product-specific reviewed `cold_exposure_rule` would be needed for a future valued risk scenario; W2 creates no default and still forbids an amount without an approved rule. `RECORDED_REPAIR` amount requires exact accepted work/cost record, currency, time and non-overlap; no such source exists in W2, so it is `UNPRICED`. All consequences pin FindingRevision, episode revision, accepted IDs, assumption revision IDs, model/formula version, source gate and overlap group. Missing/contradictory probe or policy, stale projection, mixed/unknown provenance, undeclared stock or cost â†’ `UNPRICED`/`WITHHELD` with null amount. Never sum backup and excursion exposure; no zero amount as stand-in for unknown. The existing LR current-money and S4-05 source gates still apply (`backend/migrations/versions/0010_financial_assumptions.py:1-30`; `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-69`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

All routes below use Site code, authenticated tenant scope, UTC `from,to` (end exclusive), optional UTC `known_at` (default current committed watermark), max 31-day window, and return `{site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance,limitations,...}`. `watermark` is latest included ingestion disposition `decided_at` plus assessment revision ID; a selected historical cutoff cannot claim today's currentness. Lists use opaque `(time,id)` cursor, `limit` default 50 max 200 and `{items,next_cursor}`. Shared refusal envelope `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 unauthenticated, 403 wrong tenant/source-gated disclosure, 404 Site/record not found or wrong type, 409 unresolved selected revision or recompute conflict, 422 malformed/window/range/unknown parameter, 503 ledger/projection unavailable; a valid missing claim returns 200 with `UNKNOWN`/null, never 404 or a fabricated zero. Existing evidence/Finding paths continue to serve exact links (`backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:157-161`; `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:102-189`).

### Writer guard and money trigger contract

Rule 8 in `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1`: add `cold_episode,cold_episode_revision,cold_episode_evidence` to `$assessTables` before `$assessInserts` is derived; add `cold_consequence_revision,cold_consequence_evidence,cold_consequence_work,cold_consequence_work_attempt` to `$consequenceTables`. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone inserts episode and analytic/assessment rows; `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts Findings. `assessment_worker.py` owns one caller-supplied transaction spanning both adapters and job completion. `postgres_consequence_store.py` alone inserts consequence rows and append-only work attempts; migration triggers alone insert work. The consequence writer leases with an advisory transaction lock per work ID, appends one `SUCCEEDED` attempt atomically with consequence/links or a `FAILED` attempt with error code, and never updates/deletes work or attempts. Cross-writer checks protect all four tables. The ledger alone inserts event evidence, rejection and disposition in one transaction. Every accepted-evidence INSERT uses only the message constraint conflict target; event and START unique violations are handled by per-ordinal savepoint rollback and ordered reread (message, event, session) as in Â§2. No `DO UPDATE` permission is added.

Set `$ledgerConflictAllowed` to the double-quoted PowerShell regex `"(?i)\bON\s+CONFLICT\s+ON\s+CONSTRAINT\s+(?:batch_refusal_identity|ingestion_batch_external_unique|accepted_evidence_message_unique)\s+DO\s+NOTHING\b"`; retain its "three" target error text. Do not allow an event partial-index conflict target. `ledger_reads.py` uses `kind IN ('MESSAGE_CONFLICT','EVENT_ID_CONFLICT','SESSION_ID_CONFLICT')` in both receipt count locations (lines 232 and 376), retaining existing `MESSAGE_CONFLICT` objects and bytes. No migration touches old Receipt V2 rows.

Cold money work is triggered by appended cold FindingRevision or episode revision, relevant assumption publication or source-gate change; a migration trigger inserts append-only dirty `(site,window,domain='cold_chain')` work with unique dirty key and `DO NOTHING` duplicate suppression. `financials/recompute.py` runs the consequence worker; it selects unfinished work lacking a successful attempt, locks per work ID, selects the exact settled assessment/Finding basis, writes immutable consequence/evidence rows through `postgres_consequence_store.py`, and appends a `SUCCEEDED` attempt in the same transaction. Failure appends a `FAILED` attempt with an error code, leaving work retryable; no work status UPDATE exists. `tools/writer_guard_probes.py` gains pass probes for each owner/table, message-target conflict and attempt append, and fail probes for runtime work INSERT, foreign writer, immutable UPDATE/DELETE, `DO UPDATE`, and event partial-index conflict target. Include double-collision probes: message+event must choose message; event+session with a free message must choose event. `configuration-persistence.ps1` counts probes and fails closed when an owner is absent. `overlap_group` is SHA-256 of canonical `(tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id)` prefixed `cold:`; excursion and backup consequences for that room/window share it.

### Response vocabulary

Every object includes `as_of: UTC timestamp|null` and `limitation: string|null`. Common `currentness.state` is exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`; `source_provenance` is `{classification: ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|AUTHORED|MIXED|UNKNOWN, source_ids: string[], evidence_ids: UUID[], basis_sha256: string|null}`; root `limitations` is `string[]`. `start_bounds,end_bounds,episode_bounds` are `{earliest: UTC timestamp|null,latest: UTC timestamp|null}`; `duration_bounds,degree_minute_bounds,quantity_bounds,amount_bounds` are `{low: decimal|null,high: decimal|null}`. `gaps` is `[{start:UTC timestamp,end:UTC timestamp,reason:MISSING_PROBE|SUSPECT|CLOCK_UNATTESTED|CONTRADICTORY|UNBOUND,expected_points:int,received_points:int}]`, with half-open bounds. `coverage` is a decimal in `[0,1]` plus `{known_points:int,expected_points:int}` on temperature/evidence cards.

| Field | Exact shape |
| --- | --- |
| `status` | `{state: OBSERVED_EXCURSION|NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,claim_level:OBSERVED|BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,as_of,limitation}` |
| `objective` | `{text:string,policy_low_deg_c:decimal|null,policy_high_deg_c:decimal|null,room_asset_id:UUID|null,as_of,limitation}` |
| `temperature_summary` | `{latest_deg_c:decimal|null,latest_at:UTC timestamp|null,coverage:{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal},episode_count:int,claim_level:BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,as_of,limitation}` |
| `backup_summary` | `{attempts:int,observed_success:int,observed_failure:int,unknown:int,latest_chain_id:UUID|null,as_of,limitation}` |

| `findings` | `{count:int,items:[{finding_id:UUID,revision_id:UUID,title:string,claim_level:string,link:string}],as_of,limitation}` |

## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract

Migration table correction under `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers`: `0021` also adds the thirteenth `cold-compressor-start-delay` property and exact carriers, units and bounds below. `0022` adds `cold_episode.episode_key_sha256` with unique `(tenant_id,site_id,episode_key_sha256)` and nullable scoped `split_from_episode_id`; remove the proposed unique `(site_id,kind,asset_id,anchor_time)` because distinct accepted requests can share source time. ### Architect closure: `D-2026-10-17-w2-01-answers` (controls W2-01, W2-02 and W2-03)

The following exact contract supersedes conflicting shorthand below. It is based on the closed code vocabularies in `sites/models.py`, `foundation_parsing.py`, `binding_roles.py`, `binding_service.py`, the Lab profile and mapping registries, and the S3/S4 stores. These are required additions to code in the named slices, not claims that they already exist.

**A â€” topology.** Add `COLD_ROOM`, `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`, `TRANSFER_SWITCH` to component types; add topology node roles `COLD_ROOM`, `SENSOR`, `COOLING`, `SWITCH` respectively. Existing `GENERATION`, `BUS`, `METERING` serve generator, AC bus and grid meter. Add connection media `SIGNAL` and `THERMAL` beside existing `AC|DC|FUEL`. Use `topology.connections` exclusively, with directed `COLD_ROOM -> TEMPERATURE_PROBE (SIGNAL)` for primary chamber sensing, `COMPRESSOR -> COLD_ROOM (THERMAL)` for cooling, and `METER -> TRANSFER_SWITCH -> AC_BUS -> COMPRESSOR` plus `GENERATOR -> TRANSFER_SWITCH` (`AC`). No separate relation list. Validate source/target component types for each medium and edge, unique `(from_node,to_node,medium)`, distinct endpoints and a directed acyclic graph. A missing-probe Foundation may omit the SIGNAL edge; an ambient probe has no room SIGNAL edge. At most one room-to-probe SIGNAL edge may leave a room, and at most one may enter a probe; complete readiness requires exactly one leaving each room. Refuse a probe linked to two rooms or two primaries for a room. Topology `node_id`/`connection_id` remain the existing document IDs and every referenced component must exist.

**B â€” event binding.** Add `event` to `SIGNAL_UNITS`; it is the nonquantitative declared unit of an event channel, never a numeric evidence unit. Add `EVENT` to `Role.value_kind` and give nonquantitative EVENT roles `canonical_unit='event'`, `source_unit='event'`, `conversion={kind:'IDENTITY',parameters:{}}`; extend `conversion_problem` so this exact triple is valid only for EVENT and no numeric conversion occurs. Manual temperature is the sole quantitative event channel: `source_unit=canonical_unit='degC'`, IDENTITY, `allowed_units=['degC']`, and v4 `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/degC. Other event bindings have `allowed_units=[]`, and v4 event payload `unit=value=null`; declared channel unit `event` does not appear in the envelope payload. Add explicit per-role event-kind/outcome allowlists and compare them with the five pinned v4 snapshot fields; a state ENUM conversion has a **role-specific** member set, not global `GENERATOR_STATES` (compressor OFF/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; grid AVAILABLE/LOST; generator STOPPED/STARTING/RUNNING/FAILED; bus POWERED/UNPOWERED). Reject unmapped members. Foundation policy roles stay NUMBER with `covered_by='FOUNDATION_CONTROL'`; they are selected from typed properties and have no channel/binding row.

**C â€” thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). The thirteenth property makes the stated 1-minute compressor delay a declared, frozen physical parameter. Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`. Avoid making all Celsius values positive: signed temperatures are valid. Enforce carrier types in the cold Foundation validator after parsing; do not weaken old property checks. Add `degC` to `RATING_UNITS` only for an actual declared temperature rating, never as a substitute for these properties.

**Dâ€“I â€” selected read and UI.** Until a persisted commissioning-evidence record exists, always return `commissioning:{state:'UNCONFIRMED',evidence_ids:[]}`. Gateway contact comes from the selected Site gateway-health read: `CURRENT -> RECENT`, `STALE -> STALE`, `UNKNOWN` with no diagnostic -> `NEVER`; `as_of` is the actual diagnostic receive time or null. If a diagnostic exists but its freshness is UNKNOWN, report `STALE` with limitation `Contact time cannot be verified`, never claim recent contact. This is contact, not equipment health. W2-01 publishes `bindings[].cadence_seconds=300` as the fixed `cold-roles-1` channel contract, not from stored binding rows or readings; W2-02's frozen `cold-publication/1` profile must declare the same 300-second sampling cadence and refuse a mismatch. Event channels are transition-driven and return `cadence_seconds=null`, with a `transition` publication mode. The gateway's `declared_cadence_seconds` is diagnostic heartbeat metadata and must not fill this field. For a selected COLDCHAIN Site, `/sites/:id/foundation`, `/sites/:id/foundation/readiness`, and `/sites/:id/foundation/sld` render `cold-foundation`, `cold-readiness`, `cold-sld`; MINIGRID keeps its existing views, and wrong-type cold API reads return 404. Add title-map entries `cold-readiness:2 = Gateway status Â· {selected gateway display name or 'No gateway selected'}` and `cold-sld:1 = {selected Site name} Â· {selected topology type}` when a type is declared, otherwise Site name alone. Values come from the selected read. No mock Site/gateway name is a fallback.

Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

| Role / asset type | Kind, unit, cardinality | Need |
