+**W2-01 contact null closure (`D-2026-10-17-f5-subtabs-and-contact`).** `contact.state` is never null in a successful cold Foundation/readiness response. If there is no gateway-health store or no matching selected gateway/diagnostic, return `NEVER` with `as_of:null`; do not manufacture a receive time. For a registered selected gateway with no diagnostic, the UI may say `No contact received yet`. If no gateway is selected or registered, say `No gateway selected`/`No gateway registered` and do not say that a particular gateway was never contacted. Preserve a typed limitation identifying the absent health read or gateway match. A health-read error is a read failure with an explicit unavailable limitation, not evidence for a recent or stale contact state. `NEVER` here means no contact is recorded in the available selected read; it proves neither equipment condition nor physical non-contact. Cover both missing-store and unmatched-gateway cases, including API enum, null `as_of`, and UI wording, in W2-01 review tests.
 
 **W2-02 channel closure.** Add `grid_power_event` / METER, EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, ONE, `event` unit. Replace the two impossible `backup_request` and `backup_result` channel bindings with **one** required `backup_start_event` / GENERATOR, EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, ONE; require both kinds in capability/readiness, while one registered `(generator-controller,start-event)` channel carries both. `grid_state` remains its separate `(grid-meter,state)` channel. `transfer_result`, `door_event` and `manual_temperature` keep separate channels. The W2-00 closed v4 table and W2-01 snapshot rows must name these exact roles; no channel may be bound twice. On cold panels replace status labels `Mapped`/`Not mapped` with `Configured`/`Needs a source`; use `Reporting`/`No report received` only when an actual selected evidence/contact read proves it. Keep panel titles fixed by the rendered-panel manifest.

Event equality hashes canonical source, source-time/interval timing and full payload, excluding transport/mapping/publication fields; same-message comparison keeps full-envelope SHA and wins collision precedence. Migration `0020` keys delta constraints on `measurement='INTERVAL_DELTA'`, leaving `conversion_kind` as the binding conversion. Batch V2 keeps ordinary v3 strict while a valid v4 or v3-v4-only header reaches per-ordinal parser refusal; an unusable header or cross-tenant binding selection remains a whole-batch refusal. Parser and S2 own the exact EV/correlation codes; five optional snapshot-binding keys and a closed v4 channel table define mapping compatibility. W2-00 may prove v4 with test-only injected registered binding bases; W2-01 publishes cold bindings, W2-02 registers/emits the cold runtime profile, and W2-05b registers/emits EV, an explicit W2-00 producer/profile deviation deferred to W2-02. V4 acceptance queues no dispatch work until its domain worker is introduced. The new family/kind read applies to Postgres; the legacy file route rejects those filters. This is design authority, not code, review verdict, registry promotion or owner approval.

 **C — thirteen properties.** Add exactly these definitions (carrier/type, kind, canonical unit): `cold-thermal-capacity` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/degC); `cold-heat-leak` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kW/degC); `cold-cooling-capacity` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, kW); `cold-door-heat` (COLD_ROOM, PHYSICAL, kWh/min); `cold-control-low` and `cold-control-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-policy-low` and `cold-policy-high` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-min-excursion` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, min); `cold-return-hysteresis` (COLD_ROOM, CONTROL, degC); `cold-start-timeout` (GENERATOR, CONTROL, min); `cold-probe-error` (TEMPERATURE_PROBE, PHYSICAL, degC); `cold-compressor-start-delay` (COMPRESSOR, PHYSICAL, min). Extend property `UNIT_RANGES` with inclusive `kWh/degC:[0,10000]`, `kW/degC:[0,1000]`, `kWh/min:[0,1000]`, `degC:[-40,60]`, `kW:[0,100000]`, `min:[0,100000]`; these are parser-wide finite safety bounds, while cold-specific validation requires thermal capacity/leak/cooling capacity >0, door heat >=0, compressor delay >=0, start timeout >0, probe error >=0, min excursion >0, policy/control low < high, and `0 <= hysteresis < control_high-control_low`.
